, the only case requiring verification is
\alpha=1
: by Lemma
2.5
(2)
, the class
\mathcal{I}\text{-}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{0}_{1}(\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-6.94357pt{\kappa}\kern 4.00928pt}}_{{\kern-13.15457pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.00928pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-6.94357pt{\kappa}\kern 4.00928pt}}_{{\kern-13.15457pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.00928pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-4.54819pt{\kappa}\kern 2.42052pt}}_{{\kern-8.9846pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.42052pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-3.8566pt{\kappa}\kern 1.72893pt}}_{{\kern-8.29301pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 1.72893pt}}})
is closed under arbitrary unions and under intersections of fewer than
\kappa
sets.

Proposition 4.11
.

[
1
, Proposition 4.1]

Let
\nu\in\{2,\kappa\}
, and
2^{<\kappa}=\kappa
. Given any
1\leq\alpha<\kappa^{+}
, let
\widehat{\alpha}=\kappa
if
\alpha
is a successor ordinal, and
\widehat{\alpha}=\operatorname{cof}(\alpha)
if
\alpha
is limit. Then:

(1)

\mathcal{I}\text{-}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{0}_{\alpha}(\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-6.94357pt{\kappa}\kern 4.00928pt}}_{{\kern-13.15457pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.00928pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-6.94357pt{\kappa}\kern 4.00928pt}}_{{\kern-13.15457pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.00928pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-4.54819pt{\kappa}\kern 2.42052pt}}_{{\kern-8.9846pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.42052pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-3.8566pt{\kappa}\kern 1.72893pt}}_{{\kern-8.29301pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 1.72893pt}}})
is closed under unions of length
\kappa
and intersections of size smaller than
\widehat{\alpha}
;

(2)

\mathcal{I}\text{-}\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{0}_{\alpha}(\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-6.94357pt{\kappa}\kern 4.00928pt}}_{{\kern-13.15457pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.00928pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-6.94357pt{\kappa}\kern 4.00928pt}}_{{\kern-13.15457pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.00928pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-4.54819pt{\kappa}\kern 2.42052pt}}_{{\kern-8.9846pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.42052pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-3.8566pt{\kappa}\kern 1.72893pt}}_{{\kern-8.29301pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 1.72893pt}}})
is closed under intersections of length
\kappa
and unions of size smaller than
\widehat{\alpha}
;

(3)

\mathcal{I}\text{-}\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{0}_{\alpha}(\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-6.94357pt{\kappa}\kern 4.00928pt}}_{{\kern-13.15457pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.00928pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-6.94357pt{\kappa}\kern 4.00928pt}}_{{\kern-13.15457pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.00928pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-4.54819pt{\kappa}\kern 2.42052pt}}_{{\kern-8.9846pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.42052pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-3.8566pt{\kappa}\kern 1.72893pt}}_{{\kern-8.29301pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 1.72893pt}}})
is closed under complements and both unions and intersections of size smaller than
\widehat{\alpha}
, that is,
\mathcal{I}\text{-}\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{0}_{\alpha}(\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-6.94357pt{\kappa}\kern 4.00928pt}}_{{\kern-13.15457pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.00928pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-6.94357pt{\kappa}\kern 4.00928pt}}_{{\kern-13.15457pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.00928pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-4.54819pt{\kappa}\kern 2.42052pt}}_{{\kern-8.9846pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.42052pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-3.8566pt{\kappa}\kern 1.72893pt}}_{{\kern-8.29301pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 1.72893pt}}})
is a
\widehat{\alpha}
-algebra.

We refer the reader to
[
1
]
for a detailed discussion of the optimality of the closure properties presented in
4.11
.

We conclude this section by pointing out a connection with
[
10
]
.
When one drops the assumption
\kappa^{<\kappa}=\kappa
(equivalently, that
\kappa
is regular and
2^{<\kappa}=\kappa
), there are two main approaches in the literature. The first maintains the condition
2^{<\kappa}=\kappa
but allows
\kappa
to be singular, as in
[
2
,
4
,
1
,
20
]
; this ensures that the spaces still have weight
\kappa
.
The second approach, developed in
[
10
]
, keeps
\kappa
regular while allowing
\kappa^{<\kappa}>\kappa
. This constitutes a different setup, as the spaces no longer have weight
\kappa
. In that context, the authors identify new connections with the model theory of uncountable structures by formulating an alternative notion of Borel sets, tailored to their setting and better suited for model-theoretic applications.
They begin by defining what they call a
basic
\kappa
-open set
, namely a set of the form
[f]=\{x\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-6.94357pt{\kappa}\kern 4.00928pt}}_{{\kern-13.15457pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.00928pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-6.94357pt{\kappa}\kern 4.00928pt}}_{{\kern-13.15457pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.00928pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-4.54819pt{\kappa}\kern 2.42052pt}}_{{\kern-8.9846pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.42052pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-3.8566pt{\kappa}\kern 1.72893pt}}_{{\kern-8.29301pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 1.72893pt}}}\mid f\subseteq x\}
, where
f\colon X\rightarrow\nu
and
|X|<\kappa
. As usual,
\nu\in\{2,\kappa\}
.

Definition 4.12
.

The class of
\kappa
-Borel
sets of
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-6.94357pt{\kappa}\kern 4.00928pt}}_{{\kern-13.15457pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.00928pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-6.94357pt{\kappa}\kern 4.00928pt}}_{{\kern-13.15457pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.00928pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-4.54819pt{\kappa}\kern 2.42052pt}}_{{\kern-8.9846pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.42052pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-3.8566pt{\kappa}\kern 1.72893pt}}_{{\kern-8.29301pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 1.72893pt}}}
is the smallest class containg the basic
\kappa
-open sets and closed under complements and unions of size at most
\kappa
.

In line with Definition
4.12
, one could attempt to develop the theory of Borel sets in ideal topologies using the following definition instead of the one adopted in this article (cf. page
4
).

Definition 4.13
.

The class of
basic
\mathcal{I}
-Borel
of
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-6.94357pt{\kappa}\kern 4.00928pt}}_{{\kern-13.15457pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.00928pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-6.94357pt{\kappa}\kern 4.00928pt}}_{{\kern-13.15457pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.00928pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-4.54819pt{\kappa}\kern 2.42052pt}}_{{\kern-8.9846pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.42052pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-3.8566pt{\kappa}\kern 1.72893pt}}_{{\kern-8.29301pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 1.72893pt}}}
is the smallest class containg the basic
\mathcal{I}
-open sets and closed under complements and unions of size at most
\kappa
.

As the next result shows, for any ideal
\mathcal{I}
the class of basic
\mathcal{I}
-Borel sets coincides with the class defined in Definition
4.12
.

Fact 4.14
.

For any ideal
\mathcal{I}
, the class of basic
\mathcal{I}
-Borel sets of
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-6.94357pt{\kappa}\kern 4.00928pt}}_{{\kern-13.15457pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.00928pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-6.94357pt{\kappa}\kern 4.00928pt}}_{{\kern-13.15457pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.00928pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-4.54819pt{\kappa}\kern 2.42052pt}}_{{\kern-8.9846pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.42052pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-3.8566pt{\kappa}\kern 1.72893pt}}_{{\kern-8.29301pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 1.72893pt}}}
coincide with the class of
\kappa
-Borel sets of
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-6.94357pt{\kappa}\kern 4.00928pt}}_{{\kern-13.15457pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.00928pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-6.94357pt{\kappa}\kern 4.00928pt}}_{{\kern-13.15457pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.00928pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-4.54819pt{\kappa}\kern 2.42052pt}}_{{\kern-8.9846pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.42052pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-3.8566pt{\kappa}\kern 1.72893pt}}_{{\kern-8.29301pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 1.72893pt}}}
. As a consequence, the class of basic
\mathcal{I}
-Borel is independent of the ideal
\mathcal{I}
.

Proof.

As
b\subseteq\mathcal{I}
, any basic
\kappa
-open set is a basic
\mathcal{I}
-open set, therefore every
\kappa
-Borel set is a basic
\mathcal{I}
-Borel set.
For the other inclusion, it is sufficient to show that all basic
\mathcal{I}
-open sets are
\kappa
-Borel sets. As observed in Remark
4.3
, for every
f\in\Fn_{\mathcal{I}}
,
\boldsymbol{N}_{f}=\bigcap_{\alpha\in D}\mathcal{N}^{f}_{\alpha}
, where
D=\dom(f)
and
\mathcal{N}^{f}_{\alpha}=\{x\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-6.94357pt{\kappa}\kern 4.00928pt}}_{{\kern-13.15457pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.00928pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-6.94357pt{\kappa}\kern 4.00928pt}}_{{\kern-13.15457pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.00928pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-4.54819pt{\kappa}\kern 2.42052pt}}_{{\kern-8.9846pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.42052pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-3.8566pt{\kappa}\kern 1.72893pt}}_{{\kern-8.29301pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 1.72893pt}}}\mid x(\alpha)=f(\alpha)\}
. Since each
\mathcal{N}^{f}_{\alpha}
is a basic
\kappa
-open sets and
|D|\leq\kappa
,
\boldsymbol{N}_{f}
is
\kappa
-Borel.
∎

5.
The
\mathcal{I}
-Analytic sets

In this section we introduce the notion of generalized analytic set for the spaces
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-6.92043pt{\kappa}\kern 3.98613pt}}_{{\kern-13.13142pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.98613pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-6.92043pt{\kappa}\kern 3.98613pt}}_{{\kern-13.13142pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.98613pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-4.57767pt{\kappa}\kern 2.45pt}}_{{\kern-9.01408pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.45pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-3.87767pt{\kappa}\kern 1.75pt}}_{{\kern-8.31409pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 1.75pt}}}
and
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\kappa^{{\kern-7.61604pt{\kappa}\kern 4.68175pt}}_{{\kern-13.82704pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.68175pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\kappa^{{\kern-7.61604pt{\kappa}\kern 4.68175pt}}_{{\kern-13.82704pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.68175pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\kappa^{{\kern-4.95085pt{\kappa}\kern 2.82318pt}}_{{\kern-9.38727pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.82318pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\kappa^{{\kern-4.14423pt{\kappa}\kern 2.01656pt}}_{{\kern-8.58064pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.01656pt}}}
endowed with the ideal topology
\tau_{\mathcal{I}}
. However, we immediately observe that this notion is trivial, because the class under consideration actually coincides with the entire powerset (Corollary
5.3
). Moreover, the same phenomenon occurs for the generalized versions of all the equivalent characterizations of analytic sets from classical descriptive set theory (Corollary
5.4
). As usual, fix
\nu\in\{2,\kappa\}
.

Given
\mu\in\{2,\kappa\}
, a function
\Phi\colon\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-6.94357pt{\kappa}\kern 4.00928pt}}_{{\kern-13.15457pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.00928pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-6.94357pt{\kappa}\kern 4.00928pt}}_{{\kern-13.15457pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.00928pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-4.54819pt{\kappa}\kern 2.42052pt}}_{{\kern-8.9846pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.42052pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-3.8566pt{\kappa}\kern 1.72893pt}}_{{\kern-8.29301pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 1.72893pt}}}\to\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mu^{{\kern-7.79662pt{\kappa}\kern 4.86232pt}}_{{\kern-14.00761pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.86232pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mu^{{\kern-7.79662pt{\kappa}\kern 4.86232pt}}_{{\kern-14.00761pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.86232pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mu^{{\kern-5.08015pt{\kappa}\kern 2.95248pt}}_{{\kern-9.51657pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.95248pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mu^{{\kern-4.23659pt{\kappa}\kern 2.10892pt}}_{{\kern-8.673pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.10892pt}}}

is
\mathcal{I}
-Borel
if

.

Theorem 5.2
.

If
\mathcal{I}
contains an unbounded subset of
\kappa
, then the class
\mathcal{I}\text{-}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{1}_{1}(\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-6.94357pt{\kappa}\kern 4.00928pt}}_{{\kern-13.15457pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.00928pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-6.94357pt{\kappa}\kern 4.00928pt}}_{{\kern-13.15457pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.00928pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-4.54819pt{\kappa}\kern 2.42052pt}}_{{\kern-8.9846pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.42052pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-3.8566pt{\kappa}\kern 1.72893pt}}_{{\kern-8.29301pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 1.72893pt}}})
is closed under unions of size at most
2^{\kappa}
.

Proof.

Let
\langle A_{\alpha}\mid\alpha<2^{\kappa}\rangle
be a sequence of
\mathcal{I}\text{-}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{1}_{1}
-subsets of

C
witness
\bigcup_{\alpha<2^{\kappa}}A_{\alpha}\in\mathcal{I}\text{-}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{1}_{1}(\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-6.94357pt{\kappa}\kern 4.00928pt}}_{{\kern-13.15457pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.00928pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-6.94357pt{\kappa}\kern 4.00928pt}}_{{\kern-13.15457pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.00928pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-4.54819pt{\kappa}\kern 2.42052pt}}_{{\kern-8.9846pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.42052pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-3.8566pt{\kappa}\kern 1.72893pt}}_{{\kern-8.29301pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 1.72893pt}}})
.

Notice that for every
x\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\kappa^{{\kern-7.61604pt{\kappa}\kern 4.68175pt}}_{{\kern-13.82704pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.68175pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\kappa^{{\kern-7.61604pt{\kappa}\kern 4.68175pt}}_{{\kern-13.82704pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.68175pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\kappa^{{\kern-4.95085pt{\kappa}\kern 2.82318pt}}_{{\kern-9.38727pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.82318pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\kappa^{{\kern-4.14423pt{\kappa}\kern 2.01656pt}}_{{\kern-8.58064pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.01656pt}}}
and for every
z\in\boldsymbol{N}_{x\restriction U_{0}\cup U_{1}}
,

\Phi(z)=\Phi_{\theta(x\restriction U_{1})}(\Delta(x))=\Phi(x)
, therefore
\Phi(\boldsymbol{N}_{x\restriction U_{0}\cup U_{1}})=\{\Phi(x)\}
.

The function
\Phi
is
\mathcal{I}
-continuous since for every
x\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\kappa^{{\kern-7.61604pt{\kappa}\kern 4.68175pt}}_{{\kern-13.82704pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.68175pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\kappa^{{\kern-7.61604pt{\kappa}\kern 4.68175pt}}_{{\kern-13.82704pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.68175pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\kappa^{{\kern-4.95085pt{\kappa}\kern 2.82318pt}}_{{\kern-9.38727pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.82318pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\kappa^{{\kern-4.14423pt{\kappa}\kern 2.01656pt}}_{{\kern-8.58064pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.01656pt}}}
, and for every
D\in\mathcal{I}
, the set
\boldsymbol{N}_{x\restriction U_{0}\cup U_{1}}
is
\mathcal{I}
-open (because
U_{0}\cup U_{1}\in\mathcal{I}
) and it satisfies
\Phi(\boldsymbol{N}_{x\restriction U_{0}\cup U_{1}})\subseteq\boldsymbol{N}_{\Phi(x)\restriction D}
.
To see that
C
is
\mathcal{I}
-closed we show that for every
x\notin C
,
\boldsymbol{N}_{x\restriction U_{0}\cup U_{1}}\subseteq\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\kappa^{{\kern-7.61604pt{\kappa}\kern 4.68175pt}}_{{\kern-13.82704pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.68175pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\kappa^{{\kern-7.61604pt{\kappa}\kern 4.68175pt}}_{{\kern-13.82704pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.68175pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\kappa^{{\kern-4.95085pt{\kappa}\kern 2.82318pt}}_{{\kern-9.38727pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.82318pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\kappa^{{\kern-4.14423pt{\kappa}\kern 2.01656pt}}_{{\kern-8.58064pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.01656pt}}}\setminus C
. This is immediate, as for every
y\in\boldsymbol{N}_{x\restriction U_{0}\cup U_{1}}
,
\Delta(y)=\Delta(x)
and
\theta(y\restriction U_{1})=\theta(x\restriction U_{1})
, therefore
\Delta(y)\notin C_{\theta(y\restriction U_{1})}
. The following claim concludes the proof.

Claim
.

\Phi(C)=\bigcup_{\alpha<2^{\kappa}}A_{\alpha}
.

Proof of the Claim.

If
y\in\bigcup_{\alpha<2^{\kappa}}A_{\alpha}
, then there is some
\alpha<2^{\kappa}
and some
x\in C_{\alpha}
such that
y=\Phi_{\alpha}(x)
.
Let
z\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\kappa^{{\kern-7.61604pt{\kappa}\kern 4.68175pt}}_{{\kern-13.82704pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.68175pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\kappa^{{\kern-7.61604pt{\kappa}\kern 4.68175pt}}_{{\kern-13.82704pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.68175pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\kappa^{{\kern-4.95085pt{\kappa}\kern 2.82318pt}}_{{\kern-9.38727pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.82318pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\kappa^{{\kern-4.14423pt{\kappa}\kern 2.01656pt}}_{{\kern-8.58064pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.01656pt}}}
such that
\theta(z\restriction U_{1})=\alpha
and
\Delta(z)=x
.
Then,

\Phi(z)=\Phi_{\theta(z\restriction U_{1})}(\Delta(z))=\Phi_{\alpha}(x)=y.

Finally, since
x\in C_{\alpha}
,
\Delta(z)\in C_{\theta(z\restriction U_{1})}
. Thus
z\in C
and
y\in\Phi(C)
.

For the other direction, let
y\in C
. From the definition of
C
,
\Delta(y)\in C_{\theta(y\restriction U_{1})}
. Let
\alpha=\theta(y\restriction U_{1})
and
x=\Delta(y)
. Then,
x\in C_{\alpha}
and
\Phi_{\alpha}(x)\in A_{\alpha}
. Therefore,

\Phi(y)=\Phi_{\theta(y\restriction U_{1})}(\Delta(y))=\Phi_{\alpha}(x)\in A_{\alpha}
.
∎

∎

Corollary 5.3
.

If
\mathcal{I}
contains an unbounded subset of
\kappa
, then
\mathcal{I}\text{-}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{1}_{1}(\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-6.94357pt{\kappa}\kern 4.00928pt}}_{{\kern-13.15457pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.00928pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-6.94357pt{\kappa}\kern 4.00928pt}}_{{\kern-13.15457pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.00928pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-4.54819pt{\kappa}\kern 2.42052pt}}_{{\kern-8.9846pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.42052pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-3.8566pt{\kappa}\kern 1.72893pt}}_{{\kern-8.29301pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 1.72893pt}}})=\mathscr{P}(\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-6.94357pt{\kappa}\kern 4.00928pt}}_{{\kern-13.15457pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.00928pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-6.94357pt{\kappa}\kern 4.00928pt}}_{{\kern-13.15457pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.00928pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-4.54819pt{\kappa}\kern 2.42052pt}}_{{\kern-8.9846pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.42052pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-3.8566pt{\kappa}\kern 1.72893pt}}_{{\kern-8.29301pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 1.72893pt}}})
.

The generalized Cantor and Baire spaces (equipped with the bounded topology, hence
\mathcal{I}=b
) have
2^{(2^{<\kappa})}
-many
\kappa
-analytic subsets, so the hypothesis
2^{<\kappa}=\kappa
guarantees that the class
\mathcal{I}\text{-}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{1}_{1}
is strictly smaller than the full powerset. In contrast, when
\kappa^{<\kappa}>\kappa
,
[
10
, Lemma 2.1]
shows that
\mathcal{I}\text{-}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{1}_{1}(\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-6.92043pt{\kappa}\kern 3.98613pt}}_{{\kern-13.13142pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.98613pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-6.92043pt{\kappa}\kern 3.98613pt}}_{{\kern-13.13142pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.98613pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-4.57767pt{\kappa}\kern 2.45pt}}_{{\kern-9.01408pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.45pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-3.87767pt{\kappa}\kern 1.75pt}}_{{\kern-8.31409pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 1.75pt}}})=\mathscr{P}(\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-6.92043pt{\kappa}\kern 3.98613pt}}_{{\kern-13.13142pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.98613pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-6.92043pt{\kappa}\kern 3.98613pt}}_{{\kern-13.13142pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.98613pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-4.57767pt{\kappa}\kern 2.45pt}}_{{\kern-9.01408pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.45pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-3.87767pt{\kappa}\kern 1.75pt}}_{{\kern-8.31409pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 1.75pt}}})
occurs even if
\mathcal{I}
is the bounded ideal.

Since in classical descriptive set theory the notion of analytic set can be reformulated giving several equivalent definitions
[
13
, Section 14.A]
, the reader may wonder if the generalized counterpart of any of the other definitions may be nontrivial. This is not the case, as the next result shows.

Given a subset
A\subseteq X\times Y
of a cartesian product, we denote its projection (on the first coordinate) by
\operatorname{p}(A)=\{x\in X\mid\exists y\in Y(x,y)\in A\}
.

Corollary 5.4
.

If
\mathcal{I}
contains an unbounded subset of
\kappa
, the following are equivalent:

(1)

A\in\mathscr{P}(\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-6.94357pt{\kappa}\kern 4.00928pt}}_{{\kern-13.15457pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.00928pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-6.94357pt{\kappa}\kern 4.00928pt}}_{{\kern-13.15457pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.00928pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-4.54819pt{\kappa}\kern 2.42052pt}}_{{\kern-8.9846pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.42052pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-3.8566pt{\kappa}\kern 1.72893pt}}_{{\kern-8.29301pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 1.72893pt}}})

(2)

A is
\mathcal{I}
-analytic;

(3)

-
(6)
implies
(1)
.

(4)

\mathcal{I}

\mathcal{I}

(6)

\mathcal{I}

\mathcal{I}

5.3

implies

be
\mathcal{I}
-analytic and let

Only one possible definition of
\mathcal{I}
-analytic set is missing from Corollary
5.4
.
In classical descriptive set theory (hence
\kappa=\omega
), it is well-known (see
[
13
, Section 14-15]
) that a non-empty set
A\subseteq\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-7.14726pt{\omega}\kern 3.98613pt}}_{{\kern-13.8573pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.98613pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-7.14726pt{\omega}\kern 3.98613pt}}_{{\kern-13.8573pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.98613pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-4.7397pt{\omega}\kern 2.45pt}}_{{\kern-9.5326pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.45pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-4.0397pt{\omega}\kern 1.75pt}}_{{\kern-8.8326pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 1.75pt}}}
is:

•

a continuous image of
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-8.1989pt{\omega}\kern 5.03777pt}}_{{\kern-14.90894pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 5.03777pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-8.1989pt{\omega}\kern 5.03777pt}}_{{\kern-14.90894pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 5.03777pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-5.33972pt{\omega}\kern 3.05002pt}}_{{\kern-10.13261pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.05002pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-4.46829pt{\omega}\kern 2.17859pt}}_{{\kern-9.26118pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.17859pt}}}

•

an injective continuous image of some closed

Moreover, in the generalized setting
\kappa^{<\kappa}=\kappa>\omega
, the class of continuous injective images of closed subsets of
(\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\kappa^{{\kern-7.61604pt{\kappa}\kern 4.68175pt}}_{{\kern-13.82704pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.68175pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\kappa^{{\kern-7.61604pt{\kappa}\kern 4.68175pt}}_{{\kern-13.82704pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.68175pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\kappa^{{\kern-4.95085pt{\kappa}\kern 2.82318pt}}_{{\kern-9.38727pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.82318pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\kappa^{{\kern-4.14423pt{\kappa}\kern 2.01656pt}}_{{\kern-8.58064pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.01656pt}}},\tau_{b})
consistently coincide with the class of
\kappa
-analytic sets
[
16
]
.
It is therefore natural to ask whether the generalization of at least one of these two definitions may give a nontrivial notion of
\mathcal{I}
-analytic set. The answer is negative again.

Proposition 5.5
.

Assume that
\mathcal{I}
contains an unbounded subset of
\kappa
. Then:

(1)

Every non-empty
A\subseteq\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-6.94357pt{\kappa}\kern 4.00928pt}}_{{\kern-13.15457pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.00928pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-6.94357pt{\kappa}\kern 4.00928pt}}_{{\kern-13.15457pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.00928pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-4.54819pt{\kappa}\kern 2.42052pt}}_{{\kern-8.9846pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.42052pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-3.8566pt{\kappa}\kern 1.72893pt}}_{{\kern-8.29301pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 1.72893pt}}}
is an
\mathcal{I}
-continuous image of
\ \mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\kappa^{{\kern-7.61604pt{\kappa}\kern 4.68175pt}}_{{\kern-13.82704pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.68175pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\kappa^{{\kern-7.61604pt{\kappa}\kern 4.68175pt}}_{{\kern-13.82704pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.68175pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\kappa^{{\kern-4.95085pt{\kappa}\kern 2.82318pt}}_{{\kern-9.38727pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.82318pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\kappa^{{\kern-4.14423pt{\kappa}\kern 2.01656pt}}_{{\kern-8.58064pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.01656pt}}}
.

(2)

Every
A\subseteq\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-6.94357pt{\kappa}\kern 4.00928pt}}_{{\kern-13.15457pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.00928pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-6.94357pt{\kappa}\kern 4.00928pt}}_{{\kern-13.15457pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.00928pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-4.54819pt{\kappa}\kern 2.42052pt}}_{{\kern-8.9846pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.42052pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-3.8566pt{\kappa}\kern 1.72893pt}}_{{\kern-8.29301pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 1.72893pt}}}
is an injective
\mathcal{I}
-continuous image of some
\mathcal{I}
-closed
C\subseteq\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\kappa^{{\kern-7.61604pt{\kappa}\kern 4.68175pt}}_{{\kern-13.82704pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.68175pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\kappa^{{\kern-7.61604pt{\kappa}\kern 4.68175pt}}_{{\kern-13.82704pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.68175pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\kappa^{{\kern-4.95085pt{\kappa}\kern 2.82318pt}}_{{\kern-9.38727pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.82318pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\kappa^{{\kern-4.14423pt{\kappa}\kern 2.01656pt}}_{{\kern-8.58064pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.01656pt}}}
.

be unbounded and fix a bijection

2.01656pt}}_{{\kern-10.99582pt\mathchoice{\makebox[6.1982pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.1982pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.87982pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7713pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.01656pt}}}\rightarrow\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nu^{{\kern-6.94357pt{\kappa}\kern 1.72893pt}}}
. Then

is such that for every non-empty

,
\Phi^{-1}(A)
is
\mathcal{I}
-clopen, because

and
